Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any number_theory module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 498. The projective resolution has length at most 558 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the number_theory space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 498 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 558. QED.
